Potential improvements:
1. Use exact differentiable max approximation with softmax and adaptive inverse temperature. Previous used top-k hard-ish with beta small? It used top_k and exponential weighting over top values. Our full softmax can better account constraints and use FFT.

A. FFT convolution:
```
def fft_convolve(a,b=None):
   if b is None: b=a
   n=len(a); m=len(b); size=1 << (n+m-3).bit_length()? need n+m-1.
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,size); B=np.fft.rfft(b,size); out=np.fft.irfft(A*B,size)[:n+m-1]
   return out
```
For autocorr, size next pow2 2n-1.

B. Weighted gradient G:
```
def weighted_grad(a, p):
    n=len(a)
    L=2*n-1
    prevp = p[::-1]
    size=1 << (n+L-2).bit_length()  # need n+L-1 length
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    P=np.fft.rfft(prevp,size)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(A*P,size)[:n+L-1]
    # G_i = conv[2n-2 - i]
    idx = np.arange(n)
    return conv[2*n-2 - idx]
```
Check length: n+L-1 = n+2n-1-1=3n-2. Max index 2n-2 <=3n-3 for n>=1. Min index n-1. Good.

C. Multiplicative step:
```
def mirror_step(a, p, eta, max_rel_change=1.0):
    g = 2*weighted_grad(a,p)
    F = np.dot(a,g)*0.5? Wait g=2G, F=Σ aG. μ=Σ a g =2F.
    gz = g - np.dot(a,g)
    # optional clip gz
    loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, eps)) - eta*gz
    loga -= loga.max()
    a_new = np.exp(loga)
    a_new /= a_new.sum()
    return a_new, g, gz
```

Update algorithm:
```
def smooth_optimize(a, budget, focus_list, eta_init, ...):
   loga=log(a)
   best= a
   for phase focus in schedule (e.g., 0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1,2,5,10,20,50,100,200,500)
      iterate until time or no improvement
         c=autocorr(a); M=c.max(); temp=M/focus; p=softmax(c/temp)
         G=weighted_grad(a,p); g=2G; mu=dot(a,g); gz=g-mu
         maxg=np.max(np.abs(gz))
         if maxg small break
         eta = eta_scale / maxg  # choose step ~eta_scale in log
         trials eta* [0.25,0.5,1,2,4]? compute J (or M+J) choose best
         if best metric < current metric - tol: update
         if focus large and no improvement many: break phase
      after each phase maybe best if M lower
```

Implement functions:
```
import numpy as np, time, math, sys

def pow2(n): return 1 << (int(n)-1).bit_length()
```
Be careful for n=0.

`convolve_fft_full(a, b=None)`: If b None, use a. Return np.ascontiguousarray maybe. Clipping tiny negative to 0? Convolution of nonnegative may have tiny negatives due to FFT; we can `out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)` for scoring? But gradient using negative tiny maybe fine. For accuracy, set out[out<0 & out>-1e-15]=0. Also slight positive? okay.

`softmax_weights(c, focus, mix):`
- L=len(c); cmax=c.max(); if cmax<=0 return uniform.
- temp = cmax / focus (focus>0). To avoid temp zero.
- exponent = c/temp - focus (since cmax/temp=focus). exponent=np.clip(exponent, -50, 50). If focus maybe 1000, clip -700 okay; use -50 to avoid underflow (effective zero). This means lags more than 50*temp below max ignored. Good.
- w=exp(exponent); w += floor (mix maybe 1e-6?). p=w/w.sum(); return p.
Focus: if huge, only top. Use mix=1e-8.

But if focus small (e.g. 0.1), temp=10cmax; exponent c/temp up to 0.1; weights nearly uniform. Good.

`topk_sum(c, K)`: if K>=len return sum; else np.partition(c, -K)[-K:].sum().

`metric(c, K)`: return c.max() + 1e-3*topk_sum(c,K)? Need scale. Maybe for line search use `cmax + 1e-4*sum_topK`? Let's set `top_weight = 1.0 / max(K,1)`? `avg_top = top_sum/K`. Since cmax ≥ avg_top. Use metric = cmax + 0.1*avg_top. But avg_top similar to cmax; metric ~1.1 cmax; not much. Could use variance: metric = cmax + 0.1*np.mean((c[c<cmax])?) Hard.

Alternative: For line search use active weighted sum with current weights p: `wsum = dot(p,c)`? p sum1; since sum c fixed, wsum = mean + weighted variance; not across all? Actually p sum1, dot(p,c) = E_p[c]; high p on high c, so maximizing? We want minimize dot(p,c)? p fixed, dot(p,c) is weighted sum of c but weights sum1; sum c fixed, minimizing dot(p,c) reduces high c and increases low c. It is like top_soft. For line search, use `metric = dot(p_current, c_new)` (with p from a before step). Since p emphasizes high lags, reducing metric likely reduces high lags. It has scale ~cmax. As step changes, p would change but fixed metric is quadratic. We can choose best by min `dot(p,c_new)` plus maybe actual cmax. This avoids recomputing softmax. Also track cmax.

So line search metric = dot(p, c_new) + λ * max(c_new - pmax?)? Use primary `dotp`; secondary cmax. Since p sums 1, if cmax small, dotp. But if p peaked, dotp approx cmax. Good.

During phase, after step accepted, recompute c and p.

Step size:
- Compute G, g=2G, mu=dot(a,g) (should ~2 dot(p,c)? Actually dot(a,g)=2 dot(p,c)? Since aG = dot(p,c)? Yes dot(p,c)=Σ p_k c_k = dot(a,G). μ=2F.
- gz = g - μ. Weighted mean zero.
- max_abs = np.max(np.abs(gz)) + eps. To limit relative change, `eta = step / max_abs` with step maybe 0.1 (log change max 0.1). But if gz has outliers, max large, step small. Could use `eta = step / (1.5*np.percentile(np.abs(gz),99)+eps)`? Simpler: clip `gz` to [-clip_g, clip_g] where clip_g = 10 * mean_abs? Then eta = step / max_abs_clipped. This prevents a few edge lags dominating. Use mean_abs = np.mean(np.abs(gz)) (or weighted mean). `clip = min(10*mean_abs, max_abs)` maybe. Then gz = clip(gz, -clip, clip). max_abs=clip. eta=step/max_abs.
- Step parameter `step` in log-space: maybe 0.05 to 0.2. For small focus, gradients smaller? We can set base_step=0.1.
- Trials: `[eta*0.25, eta*0.5, eta, eta*2]` maybe plus `eta*4`. To save cost, 3: 0.5,1,2. But if step too big, best small. Use 4.
- For each eta_t: loga_new = loga - eta_t*gz; normalize. Since gz weighted mean zero, sum exp maybe close; still normalize. Compute c_new, M_new, metric_new=dot(p,c_new). Pick min metric; tie lower M.
- Accept if metric_new < current_metric - 1e-15*current_metric or M_new < current_M - 1e-14. Update loga/a, count.
- If accepted: steps_no_improve=0; maybe increase step slightly? maintain.
- If not: decrease step *=0.7; if too small break phase.

Current_metric = dot(p,c) (p from current). But p sums1, dot(p,c) scale M? If cmax ~M, dot ~between mean and M. Good.

Since loga normalized each step. We also need ensure a max not too small/large. Clip loga to [-30, 30]? If loga too negative, a zero; if too positive, sum huge but normalize. We can `loga = np.clip(loga, -30, 30)` before normalize? This may introduce tiny mass in zeros; okay. To preserve sum, after normalize some a ~0. We should avoid all zero. If loga min<-50, set to -50. Max>50 set 50. Normalizing after clipping might bias but okay. Use `eps=1e-300`? loga maintain finite.

Weighted gradient computation each iteration:
- Need p length L. p_rev = p[::-1]; FFT. Use `size = next_pow2(3*n-2)`? Because convolve(a (n), p_rev(L)) length n+L-1=3n-2. `n+L-1` = 3n-2. next_pow. For n=10000 size 32768? 3n-2=29998 ->32768. Good.
- Could optimize by reusing size across repeated same n. But top-level stateless; each call computes. Could cache size? Use simple.

Potential numerical: pRev zeros at ends? If p has zeros (very small), convolution okay.

Can we compute G via direct for small K to save time? For high focus p sparse, direct over nonzero p entries may be faster than FFT for n large? But FFT okay. If p_sparse count < n/10, direct: for each k with p_k > eps, add to G via slice of a. But p length 2n; direct O(nnz * support). If K small and n large, support length for lag k up to n; direct O(K*n) maybe 50*10000=500k per iteration, FFT 32768 log ~ 500k too. FFT simpler.

Accelerate with `np.fft.rfft` and preallocated? Not needed.

Reset loga from a: `loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))`.

Perturbations:
- If stuck in a phase (no accept for e.g. 2000 iterations or time 20s), try perturb best or current. In main smooth_optimize, implement `restart_every` in iterations or time.
Perturb options:
  1. Mix current with best: a = (1-w)*best + w*current? current near best maybe.
  2. Add lognormal noise: loga += sigma * gaussian, with sigma decreasing.
  3. Rethink shape: generate one of parametric sequences length n, mix w=0.2-0.5 with best.
  4. spectral perturb: modify a's FFT magnitude to flatten, normalize.
We need top-level `random_perturb(a, best, rng, strength)` no closure.

But too much random may disrupt. Use gentle.

Focus schedule:
Maybe optimize with multiple focus epochs:
- start focus=0.2 (temp=5M, broad), step 0.1, max_iters until no improvement or time slice.
- focus=0.5,1,2,5,10,20,50,100,300.
As focus increases, metric more peaked. Could run for each focus for e.g. 20-60s. But if focus too high, stuck. Use time allocation: total budget for one length e.g. 400s. For each focus, `align_budget * weight`. More time at mid/high.
Simpler: one long loop with focus increasing whenever stall. Start focus=0.1, after each improvement maybe keep; after no improvement 1000 iterations, focus*=2. Stop focus >1000. This adapts.

Pseudo smooth_optimize:
```
def smooth_optimize(a0, time_limit, rng_seed=0, verbose=False, max_focus=1000.0):
    a = normalize(a0)
    loga = log_a(a)
    best=a; cbest=max; best_score=...
    focus=0.1
    step=0.05
    no_accept=0
    it=0
    t0=time.monotonic()
    rng=default_rng
    last_focus_change=t0
    while time<time_limit:
       c=conv(a,a); M=c.max()
       if M < bestM - tol: update best
       temp=M/focus if focus>0 else M/0.1
       p=softmax(c,temp, clip=50, floor=1e-12)
       G=weighted_grad(a,p)
       g=2*G
       mu=dot(a,g)
       gz=g-mu
       # clip gz
       mean_abs=mean(abs(gz)); if mean_abs<1e-18: break
       clip=mean_abs*8
       gz=np.clip(gz,-clip,clip)
       max_abs=max(abs(gz))
       if max_abs < 1e-20: break
       eta=step/max_abs
       base_metric=dot(p,c)
       best_trial=None
       for multi in [0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0]:
           et=eta*multi
           loga_new=loga - et*gz
           # clip loga range
           loga_new=np.clip(loga_new, -30, 30)
           # normalize via logsumexp
           m=loga_new.max(); loga_new -= m - logsumexp? Actually to normalize: loga_new -= logsumexp(loga_new); a_new=exp(loga_new). But if we use m offset:
              lse = m + log(sum(exp(loga_new-m)))
              loga_new_norm = loga_new - lse
              a_new=exp(loga_new_norm)
           c_new=conv(a_new,a_new); M_new=c_new.max(); metric_new=dot(p,c_new)
           # maybe metric for line search weighted sum plus small cmax:
           metric_new = metric_new + 1e-6*M_new? no
           choose min (metric_new, M_new)
       if best_trial metric < base_metric - 1e-15 and (M_new < M or True): accept
          loga, a = trial; no_accept=0; step=min(step*1.05,0.2)
       else:
          no_accept +=1; step=max(step*0.7,1e-3)
          if no_accept > 2000 or (time since focus_change > 30 and focus < max): focus*=2; no_accept=0; step=0.05; maybe perturb if focus large
          if step < 1e-5 and focus > 50: break
       # occasional periodic check/perturb if no improve globally for many sec
    return best
```

Detailed functions:

```
import numpy as np
import time, math

_EPS = 1e-300

def next_pow2(x):
    if x <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(x) - 1).bit_length()
```

`normalize_nonneg(a)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).ravel()
a=np.clip(a,0,None)
s=a.sum()
if not finite or s<1e-15: a=np.ones(len(a))/len(a); s=1
return a/s
```

`log_normalize(a)` returns loga: log(a) - logsumexp(loga). Use stable.
```
def log_normalize(a):
    a=np.maximum(a,1e-300)
    l=np.log(a)
    m=l.max()
    return l - (m + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(l-m))))
```

`explog_normalize(loga)`:
```
m=loga.max()
a=np.exp(loga-m)
a/=a.sum()
loga = loga - (m + np.log(a.sum())) # but a sum after exp at m? Actually log sumexp original lse = m + log(expsum). a=exp(loga-m); sum_a; loga_norm=loga - (m+log(sum_a)); a_norm=a/sum_a. Return a_norm, loga_norm.
```

# add floor

return p
```

`gradient_from_weights(a,p)`:
```

size=next_pow2(n+L-1)
A=np.fft.rfft(a,size); P=np.fft.rfft(prevp,size)
conv=np.fft.irfft(A*P,size)[:n+L-1]
idx = 2*n-2 - np.arange(n)
G=conv[idx]
# clip tiny neg
return G
```

Pseudo inside smooth_optimize:
```
def smooth_optimize(a0, time_limit, rng_seed=1, verbose=False):
    a = normalize(a0)
    loga = log_normalize(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_M=auto(a).max(); best_score=2*n*best_M
    focus=1.0
    step=0.08
    no_accept=0
    stagnant_time=0
    last_improve=time.monotonic()
    it=0
    t0=time.monotonic()
    rng=np.random.default_rng(rng_seed)
    while time-limit:
       c=autocorr(a); M=c.max()
       if M < best_M - 1e-15:
           best_M=M; best=a.copy(); last_improve=now
       # If many iterations no best? break?
       # weights
       p=softmax_weights(c, focus)
       G=gradient_from_weights(a,p)
       g=2*G
       mu=np.dot(a,g)
       gz=g-mu
       mean_abs=np.mean(np.abs(gz))
       if mean_abs < 1e-22:
           # perturb if possible
       clip=10*mean_abs
       gz=np.clip(gz,-clip,clip)
       max_abs=np.max(np.abs(gz))
       if max_abs < 1e-22: break
       eta=step/max_abs
       current_metric = M + 0.05*np.sqrt(max(c.dot(c)/len(c) - (c.sum()/len(c))**2,0))
       best_metric=inf; best_trial=None
       for mult in (0.25,0.75,2.0,4.0):
           et=eta*mult
           loga_t = loga - et*gz
           loga_t = np.clip(loga_t, -30,30)
           a_t, loga_t = normalize_log(loga_t)
           c_t=autocorr(a_t); M_t=c_t.max()
           var_t = max(c_t.dot(c_t)/L - (c_t.sum()/L)**2,0)
           metric_t = M_t + 0.05*np.sqrt(var_t)
           # To prefer score, maybe metric_t = M_t if M_t < best_M? But trial line.
           if metric_t < best_metric - 1e-18:
               best_metric=metric_t; best_trial=(a_t,loga_t,M_t)
       if best_trial is not None and (best_metric < current_metric - 1e-18 or best_trial[2] < M - 1e-18):
           a,loga,M_new=best_trial; no_accept=0
           if M_new < best_M: best...
       else:
           no_accept +=1
           step = max(step*0.75, 1e-4)
           if no_accept > 800:
               # focus up or perturb
               focus = min(focus*1.6, 1000.0)
               no_accept=0
               step=0.05
           # if focus high and stuck: perturb
           if focus > 20 and no global improve in 60s:
               a/loga = perturb; reset
       if verbose and it%1000==0: print...
       it +=1
```

Final program flow:
```
def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    def time_left(): return max(budget_s-10.0 - (time.monotonic()-t0), 1.0) # but no closure! Rules no closures from function nesting? It says all helper functions top level and no closures from function nesting. Inside propose_candidate nested functions are closures? It says Make all helper functions top level and have no closures from function nesting. So we must not define helper functions inside propose_candidate. No lambdas. We can compute time_left inline.

    best_seq=None; best_score=inf
    # load parent
    try:
       p=np.asarray(height_sequence_1,float)
       p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
       if p...:
          best_seq=normalize(p)
          best_score=local_score(best_seq)
          print...
    except: pass
    if best_seq is None:
       # generate parametric best at n=6000
       n0=6000

Let's outline `make_weights(c, mode, tau_frac, focus)`:
```
cmax=c.max(); cmean=c.mean()
if mode == 'var':
    w = c - cmean
elif mode == 'hinge':
    tau = cmean + tau_frac*(cmax-cmean)
    w = np.maximum(c - tau, 0.0)
    if w.max() <= 0: w = c - cmean
elif mode == 'soft':
    w = softmax_weights(c, focus) * len(c) # scale? Not needed; max ~ maybe 1. For consistency normalize max=1: w=softmax; w/w.max()
wmax=np.max(np.abs(w))
if not finite or wmax<eps: return c - cmean normalized
return w/wmax
```
For soft, softmax sum1 max maybe L; scaling to max1 makes weights broad. Good.

       g=2G; mu=dot(a,g); gz=g-mu
       ... line search

```

Line search and normalization details:
```
def normalize_from_log(loga):
    l = np.clip(loga, -30, 30)
    m = l.max()
    a = np.exp(l - m)
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not finite: return np.ones(len(l))/len(l), log_normalize(...)
    a /= s
    # recompute loga normalized: (l - m) - log(s)
    l_norm = (l - m) - np.log(s)
    return a, l_norm
```
This ensures a sum1 and loga consistent. If loga has huge min, clipping to -30 means some tiny uniform floor. Good.

When updating `loga` as `loga - et*gz`, gz may have values ~1 (if weights scaled) not 1/n. Then et=step/max_abs_gz? Scaling weights is beneficial.

```
def optimize_gradient(a0, time_limit, mode='var', param_start=0.0, param_end=0.0, step_init=0.12, seed=0, verbose=False, check_eval_every=60):
    n=len(a0)
    a=normalize(a0)
    loga=log_normalize(a)
    c=autocorr(a); M=c.max(); best_M=...
    best=a.copy()
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    step=step_init
    param=param_start
    t0=time.monotonic()
    last_accept=t0
    last_best=t0
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
       if it>0: c=autocorr(a); M=... else reuse? We already have c.
       if M < best_M - 1e-15: best...
       # if not improving best for 90s and step small? perturb/reset
       w=make_weights(c, mode, param)
       G=gradient_from_weights(a,w)
       g=2*G
       mu=np.dot(a,g)
       gz=g-mu
       mean_abs=np.mean(np.abs(gz))
       if mean_abs<1e-24:
           # reset
       clip=8*mean_abs
       gz=np.clip(gz,-clip,clip)
       max_abs=np.max(np.abs(gz))
       if max_abs<1e-24: break
       eta=step/max_abs
       cur_metric=metric(c)
       best_metric=cur_metric; best_new=None
       for mult in [0.25,0.75,1.5,3.0]:
          ...
       if best_new and (best_metric < cur_metric - 1e-24): accept
          ...
          step=min(step*1.08,0.35)
          last_accept=now
       else:
          step=max(step*0.7,1e-4)
          if now-last_accept>15:
             # advance param
             if mode == 'soft': param = min(max(param*1.5,1.0), param_end)
             elif mode == 'hinge': param = min(param + 0.07, param_end)
             else: param=0
             last_accept=now
             step=max(step_init*0.5, step)
             if param >= param_end and step < 1e-3:
                 # perturb/reset
                 loga = log_normalize(best)
                 loga += rng.normal(0,0.03,n)
                 step=step_init
                 param=param_start if mode!='soft' else 1
             if best_M not improve for 120: reset best
       it+=1
       if verbose and (it%2000==0 or time-last_print>10): print
    return best
```
Need `param_end` default for var maybe 1.

Metric function: Need compute variance. Since c sum maybe not exactly 1 if a normalized to sum1 but FFT errors. Use:
```
def metric_score(c):
    M=float(c.max())
    if c.size==0: return M
    mean=float(c.mean())
    var=float(np.dot(c,c)/c.size - mean*mean)
    if var<0: var=0
    return M + 0.1*math.sqrt(var)
```
 math imported.

Could `np.dot(c,c)` for complex? c real. Good.

Maybe negative gz and loga: If gz weighted mean zero, `loga - et*gz` may have large positive for negative gz. Clipping to 30 controls.

Now, `make_weights` implementation:
```
def make_weights(c, mode, param):
    L=len(c)
    cmax=float(c.max()); cmean=float(c.mean())
    if mode == 'var':
       w=c-cmean
    elif mode == 'hinge':
       t=float(np.clip(param,0.0,1.0))
       tau=cmean + t*(cmax-cmean)
       w=np.maximum(c-tau,0.0)
       if w.max() <= 0: w=c-cmean
    elif mode == 'soft':
       p=softmax_weights(c, max(float(param),0.1))
       w=p
    else: w=c-cmean
    wmax=float(np.max(np.abs(w)))
    if not finite or wmax<1e-30:
       w=c-cmean; wmax=...
    return w/wmax
```
For soft, p sum1, max maybe 1 if concentrated. Scale max1. Good.

Softmax_weights uses focus; if focus is param maybe up to 1000. If param ends 500. If low, broad.

Mode 'hinge' with tau_frac 0: w=max(c-cmean,0); param 1: w=max(c-cmax,0) basically only max lag(s). For cmax unique, w very peaked. Good.

Parameter progression in optimize for hinge: param_start maybe 0.3, end0.98. If no accept advance by 0.06. For soft focus: start5, end500, multiply 1.4.

def run_optimizer_phases(a0, time_limit, seed=0, verbose=False):

Pseudo code for optimize_gradient:
```
def optimize_gradient(...):
    t0=...
    a=normalize_nonneg(a0)
    loga=log_normalize(a)
    best=a.copy()
    c=autocorr_fft(a); best_M=float(c.max()); best_score=2*n*best_M
    step=step_init; param=param_start; last_accept=t0; last_best=t0; last_print=t0; it=0
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
        c=autocorr_fft(a); M=float(c.max())
        if not finite: break
        if M < best_M - 1e-15:
            best_M=M; best=a.copy(); last_best=now
        w=make_weights(c, mode, param)
        G=gradient_from_weights(a,w)
        g=2.0*G
        mu=float(np.dot(a,g))
        gz=g-mu
        mean_abs=float(np.mean(np.abs(gz)))
        if mean_abs < 1e-25:
            # perturb reset
            loga, a = ...; continue
        clip=8.0*mean_abs
        gz=np.clip(gz,-clip,clip)
        max_abs=float(np.max(np.abs(gz)))
        if max_abs < 1e-25: break
        eta=step/max_abs
        cur_metric=metric_from_c(c)
        best_trial_metric=cur_metric; best_trial_M=M; best_tri_loga=None
        for mult in (0.25,0.75,1.5,3.0):
            et=eta*mult
            loga_try=loga - et*gz
            a_try, loga_try = normalize_log(loga_try)
            c_try=autocorr_fft(a_try); M_try=float(c_try.max())
            metric_try=metric_from_c(c_try)
            if metric_try < best_trial_metric - 1e-20 or (abs(metric_try-best_trial_metric)<=1e-20 and M_try<best_trial_M-1e-20):
                 update
        if best_tri_loga is not None and (best_trial_metric < cur_metric - 1e-20 or best_trial_M < M - 1e-18):
             a, loga = ...; no_accept=0; step=min(step*1.07, step_max); last_accept=now
        else:
             no_accept +=1; step=max(step*0.72, 1e-4)
             now=...
             if now-last_accept > 12.0:
                 # advance param or reset
                 if mode == 'soft': param = min(max(param*1.5, 1.0), param_end)
                 elif mode == 'hinge': param = min(param + 0.08, param_end)
                 # var no
                 last_accept=now
                 if param >= param_end and step < 1e-3:
                    # reset to best with noise
                    loga = log_normalize(best)
                    noise = 0.02 + 0.08*(1.0 - min(1.0, step/step_init))
                    loga = loga + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n)
                    a, loga = normalize_log(loga)
                    step = step_init
                    param = param_start
                    no_accept=0
                 elif param >= param_end:
                    # perturb current
                    loga += rng.normal(0.0,0.02,n); a,loga=normalize_log(loga); ...
        it +=1
        if verbose and (it%2000==0 or now-last_print>10): print
    return best
```

def next_pow2(x):
    x=int(x)
    if x<=1: return 1
    return 1 << (x-1).bit_length()
```

Detailed code for optimize_gradient:
```
def optimize_gradient(a0, time_limit, mode='var', param_start=0.0, param_end=1.0, step_init=0.12, seed=0, verbose=False):
    if time_limit <= 0.5: return normalize_nonneg(a0)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    n=len(a0)
    a=normalize_nonneg(a0)
    loga=log_normalize(a)
    best=a.copy()
    c=autocorr_fft(a); best_M=float(c.max()); best_score=2*n*best_M
    step=step_init
    step_max=0.35
    param=param_start
    last_accept=t0; last_best=t0; last_print=t0; it=0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
        now=time.monotonic()
        if now-t0 > time_limit: break
        c=autocorr_fft(a)
        M=float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(M): break
        if M < best_M - 1e-15:
            best_M=M; best=a.copy(); last_best=now
        if now-last_best > 90.0 and M > best_M*1.001:
            loga=log_normalize(best)
            noise=0.05+0.1*rng.random()
            loga=loga+rng.normal(0.0,noise,n)
            a,loga=normalize_log(loga)
            step=step_init
            param=param_start
            last_best=now
        w=make_weights(c, mode, param)
        G=gradient_from_weights(a,w)
        g=2.0*G
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            a=best.copy(); loga=log_normalize(a); continue
        mu=float(np.dot(a,g))
        gz=g-mu
        mean_abs=float(np.mean(np.abs(gz)))
        if not np.isfinite(mean_abs) or mean_abs < 1e-25:
            loga=log_normalize(best)
            loga=loga+rng.normal(0.0,0.05,n)
            a,loga=normalize_log(loga)
            step=step_init
            param=param_start
            continue
        clip=8.0*mean_abs
        gz=np.clip(gz,-clip,clip)
        max_abs=float(np.max(np.abs(gz)))
        if max_abs < 1e-25: break
        eta=step/max_abs
        cur_metric=metric_from_c(c)
        best_trial_metric=cur_metric
        best_trial_M=M
        best_trial_loga=None
        best_trial_a=None
        for mult in (0.25,0.75,1.5,3.0):
            et=eta*mult
            loga_try=loga - et*gz
            a_try, loga_try_norm=normalize_log(loga_try)
            c_try=autocorr_fft(a_try)
            M_try=float(c_try.max())
            m_try=metric_from_c(c_try)
            if (m_try < best_trial_metric - 1e-20) or (abs(m_try-best_trial_metric)<=1e-20 and M_try < best_trial_M - 1e-20):
                best_trial_metric=m_try; best_trial_M=M_try; best_trial_a=a_try; best_trial_loga=loga_try_norm
        if best_trial_a is not None and (best_trial_metric < cur_metric - 1e-20 or best_trial_M < M - 1e-18):
            a=best_trial_a; loga=best_trial_loga
            if M? Need update best if best_trial_M < best_M:
               if best_trial_M < best_M - 1e-15: best=best_trial_a.copy(); best_M=best_trial_M; last_best=time.monotonic()
            step=min(step*1.08, step_max)
            last_accept=time.monotonic()
        else:
            step=max(step*0.72, 1e-4)
            now=time.monotonic()
            if now-last_accept > 10.0:
                if mode == 'soft':
                    if param < param_end:
                        param = min(max(param*1.5, 1.0), param_end)
                    else:
                        # reset/explore
                        loga=log_normalize(best)
                        noise=0.05+0.1*rng.random()
                        loga=loga+rng.normal(0.0,noise,n)
                        a,loga=normalize_log(loga)
                        step=step_init
                        param=param_start
                elif mode == 'hinge':
                    if param < param_end:
                        param = min(param + 0.08, param_end)
                    else:
                        loga=log_normalize(best)
                        noise=0.05+0.1*rng.random()
                        loga=loga+rng.normal(0.0,noise,n)
                        a,loga=normalize_log(loga)
                        step=step_init
                        param=param_start
                else:
                    # var: reset/explore
                    loga=log_normalize(best)
                    noise=0.05+0.1*rng.random()
                    loga=loga+rng.normal(0.0,noise,n)
                    a,loga=normalize_log(loga)
                    step=step_init
                last_accept=time.monotonic()
        it +=1
        if verbose and (it % 2000 ==0 or now-last_print>10.0):
            score=2.0*n*best_M
            print(f"      opt mode={mode} n={n} t={now-t0:.0f}s score={score:.6f} M={best_M:.3e} param={param:.3f} step={step:.4f}")
            last_print=now
    return best
```
Need update best after accept if `best_trial_M` lower even if metric not? We do. Also if current M at top of loop updates best before trials. Good.

If accepted, `last_best` updates if trial M lower; but if M not lower no.

Potential bug: In accept branch, if best_trial_a is a view? normalize_log returns new array. Good. Copy for best.

def run_optimizer_phases(a0, time_limit, seed=0, verbose=False, include_var=True):
    if time_limit <=1: return normalize_nonneg(a0)
    t0=time.monotonic(); a=normalize_nonneg(a0); best=a; best_score=local_score_arr(a)
    # helper inline update via local variables

       t1=min(rem*0.35, 240.0)
       a1=optimize_gradient(a, t1, mode='var', param_start=0.0, param_end=1.0, step_init=0.12, seed=seed, verbose=verbose)
       s1=local_score_arr(a1)
       if s1<best_score: best=a1; best_score=s1

    rem=time_limit-(time.monotonic()-t0)

We can add a final `pair_descent` top-level and call in final polish. Let's design quick pair descent using gradient from hinge/soft:
- Load a best, compute c, M. Choose weights w=hinge tau=0.9M maybe, gradient g=2G, centered gz. The direction to reduce metric: move mass from high gz to low gz. We can perform greedy transfers:
   * sources: top 20 indices with high a_i * gz_i (or gz_i). destinations: low gz_j.
   * For each source-sink pair, try transfer fractions; evaluate local score via FFT; accept if M improves (or metric). Repeat until time.
This is similar previous but more directed. It can use full gradient. It may find improvements that full step can't due to nonlinearity.

Implement `pair_descent(a0, time_limit, seed, verbose)`:
```
def pair_descent(a0, time_limit, seed=0, verbose=False):
   a=normalize; best=a; best_M=...
   t0; rng
   while time:
      c=autocorr(a); M=c.max(); update best
      w=make_weights(c, 'hinge', 0.9)
      G=gradient_from_weights(a,w); g=2G; mu=dot(a,g); gz=g-mu
      # sources: top a*positive gz? We want decrease metric by moving mass from high gz to low gz because update loga -= eta gz. In a-transfer, direction e_dst - e_src changes metric derivative = gz_dst - gz_src. To reduce, need gz_dst < gz_src. Choose src with high gz and positive a; dst low gz.
      src_indices = top 30 by gz (maybe weighted a)
      dst_indices = bottom 30 by gz
      for src in src_indices:
         for dst in dst_indices:
            if a[src] < 1e-12: continue
            for frac in [0.01,0.03,0.05,0.1,0.2]:
                amt=a[src]*frac
                a_try=a.copy(); a_try[src]-=amt; a_try[dst]+=amt; if negative clip normalize
                M_try=autocorr(a_try).max()
                if M_try < best_M - 1e-15: accept; break
      if no improvement many, perturb
```

def pair_descent(a0, time_limit, seed=0, verbose=False):
    a=normalize; best=a; best_M=local max; t0; rng; no=0
    while time:
       c=autocorr; M=...

       w=make_weights(c,'hinge',0.85)
       G=grad; g=2G; mu=dot; gz=g-mu
       # Only if finite.
       order=np.argsort(gz)
       dsts=order[:8]; srcs=order[-8:]
       improved=False
       for si in srcs:
          for di in dsts:
             if si==di or a[si]<=1e-14: continue
             for frac in (0.01,0.03,0.08,0.15):
                amt=a[si]*frac
                a_try=a.copy()
                a_try[si]-=amt; a_try[di]+=amt
                a_try=np.maximum(a_try,0); s=a_try.sum(); if s<1e-15 break; a_try/=s

             if improved: break
          if improved: break

          # perturb
          if no % 5 ==0:
             a=best.copy(); a += rng.normal(0,1e-4,a.size); normalize; no=0
          if no>50: break
       if verbose...
    return best

```python
"""
Minimize 2*n*max(convolve(a,a))/(sum(a)^2) for non-negative sequences.

Strategy:
- Work with sum(a)=1, so the score is 2*n*max(a*a).
- Use FFT-weighted gradient directions derived from scalarizations of the
  convolution vector:
      * variance-like signed weights (fast global flattening)
      * hinge/top-lag weights (focused peak reduction)
      * softmax weights (very peaked final polishing)
- Mirror/log-rate line search with adaptive step and restarts.
- Multi-length exploration from parent/interpolated/parametric starting points.
- Final local pair moves to squeeze out residual peak improvements.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math


def next_pow2(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_nonneg(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        a = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def log_normalize(a):
    a = np.maximum(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel(), 1e-300)
    l = np.log(a)
    m = float(l.max())
    e = np.exp(l - m)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(a.shape, -math.log(float(a.size)), dtype=np.float64)
    return l - (m + float(np.log(s)))


def normalize_from_log(loga):
    loga = np.asarray(loga, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = loga.size
    loga = np.clip(loga, -30.0, 30.0)
    m = float(loga.max())
    a = np.exp(loga - m)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        l = np.full(n, -math.log(float(n)), dtype=np.float64)
        return a, l
    a = a / s
    l = loga - (m + float(np.log(s)))
    return a, l


def autocorr_fft(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    size = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, size)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def convolve_fft(a, b):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    m = b.size
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    size = next_pow2(n + m - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    B = np.fft.rfft(b, size)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * B, size)[:n + m - 1]
    return np.asarray(out, dtype=np.float64)


def gradient_from_weights(a, w):
    """
    Given weights w_k for k=0..2n-2, compute G_i = sum_j w_{i+j} a_j.
    """
    n = a.size
    L = w.size
    if n == 0 or L == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    wr = w[::-1].copy()
    conv = convolve_fft(a, wr)
    # G_i = conv[2n-2-i]
    idx = 2 * n - 2 - np.arange(n)
    return conv[idx]


def softmax_weights(c, focus):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    L = c.size
    if L == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if cmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
    focus = max(float(focus), 0.1)
    temp = cmax / focus
    if temp <= 1e-300:
        temp = 1e-300
    ex = c / temp - focus
    ex = np.clip(ex, -50.0, 0.0)
    p = np.exp(ex)
    p += 1e-15
    p = p / float(p.sum())
    return p


def make_weights(c, mode, param):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    cmean = float(c.mean())
    if c.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(cmax) or not np.isfinite(cmean):
        return c.copy()

    if mode == "var":
        w = c - cmean
    elif mode == "hinge":
        t = float(np.clip(param, 0.0, 1.0))
        tau = cmean + t * (cmax - cmean)
        w = np.maximum(c - tau, 0.0)
        if float(w.max()) <= 1e-300:
            w = c - cmean
    elif mode == "soft":
        p = softmax_weights(c, max(float(param), 1.0))
        w = p
    else:
        w = c - cmean

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(w)):
        w = c - cmean

    wmax = float(np.max(np.abs(w)))
    if wmax <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(wmax):
        w = c - cmean
        wmax = float(np.max(np.abs(w)))
        if wmax <= 1e-300:
            return np.zeros_like(w)
    return w / wmax


def metric_from_c(c):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    M = float(c.max())
    if M < 0.0 and M > -1e-30:
        M = 0.0
    mean = float(np.sum(c)) / float(c.size)
    var = float(np.dot(c, c)) / float(c.size) - mean * mean
    if var < 0.0 or not np.isfinite(var):
        var = 0.0
    return float(M) + 0.20 * math.sqrt(var)


def local_score_arr(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    c = autocorr_fft(a)
    return float(2.0 * a.size * c.max())


def consider_candidate(f, best_a, best_score):
    a = normalize_nonneg(np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64))
    sc = local_score_arr(a)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
        return a, sc
    return best_a, best_score


def best_parametric_seed(n, limit_time=20.0, seed=1):
    """
    Quick structured + random spectral initialization.
    """
    n = int(max(50, n))
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    if limit_time <= 0.0:
        limit_time = 1.0

    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    two_pi_x = 2.0 * np.pi * x

    best_a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    best_score = local_score_arr(best_a)

    def time_break():
        return (time.monotonic() - t0) > 0.8 * float(max(limit_time, 1.0))

    # Symmetric/asymmetric sine, cosine, low-order spectral, powers, exp, Gaussians.
    for alpha in (0.0, 0.2, 0.35, 0.5, 0.6, 0.7, 0.8, 0.9, 0.95, 1.0):
        f = 1.0 + alpha * np.sin(two_pi_x)
        best_a, best_score = consider_candidate(f, best_a, best_score)
        if time_break():
            break
        f = 1.0 - alpha * np.sin(two_pi_x)
        best_a, best_score = consider_candidate(f, best_a, best_score)
        if time_break():
            break

    amp = (0.3, 0.5, 0.7, 0.9)
    phis = (0.0, 0.35, 0.7, 1.05, 1.4, 1.75, 2.1, 2.45, 2.8, 3.15)
    for a1 in amp:
        for ph in phis:
            if time_break():
                break
            f = 1.0 + a1 * np.cos(two_pi_x + ph)
            best_a, best_score = consider_candidate(f, best_a, best_score)

    # Random low-order spectral perturbations.
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    random_iter = 28
    if limit_time > 8.0:
        random_iter = 40
    for _ in range(random_iter):
        if time_break():
            break
        f = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        f += 0.75 * rng.normal() * np.sin(two_pi_x)
        f += 0.75 * rng.normal() * np.cos(two_pi_x)
        f += 0.35 * rng.normal() * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * x)
        f += 0.35 * rng.normal() * np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * x)
        f += 0.18 * rng.normal() * np.sin(6.0 * np.pi * x)
        best_a, best_score = consider_candidate(f, best_a, best_score)

    # Power/asymmetric shapes.
    exponents = (0.3, 0.5, 0.7, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0)
    for p in exponents:
        if time_break():
            break
        f = (x + 1e-12) ** p
        best_a, best_score = consider_candidate(f, best_a, best_score)
        f = (1.0 - x + 1e-12) ** p
        best_a, best_score = consider_candidate(f, best_a, best_score)
        for w in (0.2, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0, 2.0):
            if time_break():
                break
            f = (x + 1e-12) ** p + w * (1.0 - x + 1e-12) ** p
            best_a, best_score = consider_candidate(f, best_a, best_score)

    # Exponential asymmetries.
    for r in (1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        if time_break():
            break
        f = np.exp(-r * x)
        best_a, best_score = consider_candidate(f, best_a, best_score)
        f = np.exp(r * x)
        best_a, best_score = consider_candidate(f, best_a, best_score)

    # Gaussians.
    for ctr in (0.1, 0.25, 0.4, 0.5, 0.6, 0.75, 0.9):
        for sig in (0.1, 0.18, 0.28, 0.42):
            if time_break():
                break
            f = np.exp(-((x - ctr) ** 2) / (2.0 * sig * sig))
            best_a, best_score = consider_candidate(f, best_a, best_score)

    # A few random smooth positive functions.
    for _ in range(14):
        if time_break():
            break
        f = rng.random(n)
        f *= np.exp(0.5 * rng.normal(size=n))
        f += 0.05
        best_a, best_score = consider_candidate(f, best_a, best_score)

    return best_a


def interpolate_sequence(a, new_n):
    a = normalize_nonneg(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64))
    old_n = a.size
    new_n = int(max(1, new_n))
    if old_n == new_n:
        return a.copy()
    if old_n == 0:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(new_n)

    x_old = (np.arange(old_n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(old_n)
    x_new = (np.arange(new_n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(new_n)
    f = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return normalize_nonneg(f)


def optimize_gradient(a0, time_limit, mode="var", param_start=0.0,
                      param_end=1.0, step_init=0.12, seed=0, verbose=False):
    """
    Local mirror/log update with adaptive weights and line search.
    mode:
      var  : signed variance weights c - mean(c)
      hinge: positive weights max(c - tau, 0), tau = mean + param*(max-mean)
      soft : softmax weights with focus=param
    """
    if time_limit <= 0.5:
        return normalize_nonneg(a0)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    a = normalize_nonneg(a0)
    n = a.size
    loga = log_normalize(a)

    best = a.copy()
    c0 = autocorr_fft(a)
    best_M = float(c0.max())
    if not np.isfinite(best_M):
        best_M = 0.0

    step = float(step_init)
    step_max = 0.35
    step_min = 1e-4
    param = float(param_start)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    last_accept = t0
    last_best = t0
    last_print = t0
    it = 0

    while True:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now - t0 >= float(time_limit):
            break

        c = autocorr_fft(a)
        M = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(M):
            break

        if M < best_M - 1e-15:
            best_M = M
            best = a.copy()
            last_best = now

        # If the current trajectory is drifting away from best, reset with small noise.
        if now - last_best > 90.0 and M > best_M * 1.001:
            loga = log_normalize(best)
            noise = 0.04 + 0.10 * float(rng.random())
            loga = loga + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n)
            a, loga = normalize_from_log(loga)
            step = float(step_init)
            param = float(param_start)
            last_best = now

        w = make_weights(c, mode, param)
        G = gradient_from_weights(a, w)
        g = 2.0 * G
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            a = best.copy()
            loga = log_normalize(a)
            continue

        mu = float(np.dot(a, g))
        gz = g - mu
        mean_abs = float(np.mean(np.abs(gz)))
        if not np.isfinite(mean_abs) or mean_abs < 1e-25:
            loga = log_normalize(best)
            loga += rng.normal(0.0, 0.04, n)
            a, loga = normalize_from_log(loga)
            step = float(step_init)
            param = float(param_start)
            continue

        clip = 6.0 * mean_abs
        gz = np.clip(gz, -clip, clip)
        max_abs = float(np.max(np.abs(gz)))
        if max_abs < 1e-25 or not np.isfinite(max_abs):
            break

        eta = step / max_abs
        cur_metric = metric_from_c(c)

        best_trial_metric = cur_metric
        best_trial_M = M
        best_trial_a = None
        best_trial_loga = None

        # Backtracking / expanding line search in log-space.
        for mult in (0.25, 0.75, 1.5, 3.0):
            et = eta * mult
            loga_try = loga - et * gz
            a_try, loga_try = normalize_from_log(loga_try)
            c_try = autocorr_fft(a_try)
            M_try = float(c_try.max())
            m_try = metric_from_c(c_try)

            better = False
            if m_try < best_trial_metric - 1e-20:
                better = True
            elif abs(m_try - best_trial_metric) <= 1e-20 and M_try < best_trial_M - 1e-20:
                better = True

            if better:
                best_trial_metric = m_try
                best_trial_M = M_try
                best_trial_a = a_try
                best_trial_loga = loga_try

        now = time.monotonic()
        if best_trial_a is not None and (
                best_trial_metric < cur_metric - 1e-20 or
                best_trial_M < M - 1e-18):
            a = best_trial_a
            loga = best_trial_loga
            if best_trial_M < best_M - 1e-15:
                best_M = best_trial_M
                best = a.copy()
                last_best = now
            step = min(step * 1.08, step_max)
            last_accept = now
        else:
            step = max(step * 0.72, step_min)
            if now - last_accept > 10.0:
                if mode == "soft":
                    if param < param_end:
                        param = min(max(param * 1.5, 1.0), float(param_end))
                    else:
                        loga = log_normalize(best)
                        noise = 0.04 + 0.12 * float(rng.random())
                        loga += rng.normal(0.0, noise, n)
                        a, loga = normalize_from_log(loga)
                        step = float(step_init)
                        param = float(param_start)
                elif mode == "hinge":
                    if param < param_end:
                        param = min(param + 0.08, float(param_end))
                    else:
                        loga = log_normalize(best)
                        noise = 0.04 + 0.12 * float(rng.random())
                        loga += rng.normal(0.0, noise, n)
                        a, loga = normalize_from_log(loga)
                        step = float(step_init)
                        param = float(param_start)
                else:
                    loga = log_normalize(best)
                    noise = 0.04 + 0.12 * float(rng.random())
                    loga += rng.normal(0.0, noise, n)
                    a, loga = normalize_from_log(loga)
                    step = float(step_init)
                    param = float(param_start)

                last_accept = now

        it += 1
        if verbose and (it % 2000 == 0 or now - last_print > 10.0):
            score = 2.0 * float(n) * best_M
            print(f"      opt mode={mode} n={n} t={now-t0:.0f}s "
                  f"score={score:.6f} M={best_M:.3e} param={param:.3f} step={step:.4f}")
            last_print = now

    return best


def pair_descent(a0, time_limit, seed=0, verbose=False):
    """
    Final greedy two-point transfer polish. Uses a hinge-gradient direction
    to identify likely source/destination indices, then exact FFT checks.
    """
    if time_limit <= 2.0:
        return normalize_nonneg(a0)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    a = normalize_nonneg(a0)
    n = a.size
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    c = autocorr_fft(a)
    best_M = float(c.max())
    best = a.copy()
    stall = 0

    while True:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now - t0 >= float(time_limit):
            break

        c = autocorr_fft(a)
        M = float(c.max())
        if M < best_M - 1e-15:
            best_M = M
            best = a.copy()

        w = make_weights(c, "hinge", 0.85)
        G = gradient_from_weights(a, w)
        g = 2.0 * G
        mu = float(np.dot(a, g))
        gz = g - mu
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(gz)):
            break

        order = np.argsort(gz)
        dsts = order[:8]
        srcs = order[-8:]

        improved = False
        for si in srcs:
            si = int(si)
            if a[si] <= 1e-14:
                continue
            for di in dsts:
                di = int(di)
                if di == si:
                    continue
                for frac in (0.005, 0.015, 0.03, 0.07, 0.13):
                    amt = a[si] * frac
                    if amt <= 1e-30:
                        continue
                    try = a.copy()
                    try[si] -= amt
                    try[di] += amt
                    try = np.maximum(try, 0.0)
                    ss = float(try.sum())
                    if ss <= 1e-30:
                        continue
                    try = try / ss
                    c_try = autocorr_fft(try)
                    M_try = float(c_try.max())
                    if M_try < best_M - 1e-15:
                        a = try
                        best = a.copy()
                        best_M = M_try
                        improved = True
                        break
                if improved:
                    break
            if improved:
                break

        if not improved:
            stall += 1
            if stall % 7 == 0:
                a = best.copy()
                a += rng.normal(0.0, 1e-4, n)
                a = normalize_nonneg(a)
            if stall > 70:
                break
        else:
            stall = 0

        if verbose and now - t0 > 10.0:
            print(f"      pair n={n} t={now-t0:.0f}s score={2.0*n*best_M:.6f}")

    return best


def run_optimizer_phases(a0, time_limit, seed=0, verbose=False, include_var=True):
    if time_limit <= 1.0:
        return normalize_nonneg(a0)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    a = normalize_nonneg(a0)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = local_score_arr(a)

    rem = float(time_limit) - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if include_var and rem > 15.0:
        t1 = min(rem * 0.35, 240.0)
        a1 = optimize_gradient(a, t1, mode="var",
                               param_start=0.0, param_end=1.0,
                               step_init=0.12, seed=seed, verbose=verbose)
        s1 = local_score_arr(a1)
        if np.isfinite(s1) and s1 < best_score:
            best = a1.copy()
            best_score = s1

    rem = float(time_limit) - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if rem > 15.0:
        frac_h = 0.50 if include_var else 0.45
        t2 = min(rem * frac_h, 300.0)
        a2 = optimize_gradient(best, t2, mode="hinge",
                               param_start=0.25, param_end=0.97,
                               step_init=0.09, seed=seed + 17, verbose=verbose)
        s2 = local_score_arr(a2)
        if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score:
            best = a2.copy()
            best_score = s2

    rem = float(time_limit) - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    if rem > 10.0:
        a3 = optimize_gradient(best, rem, mode="soft",
                               param_start=5.0, param_end=1000.0,
                               step_init=0.06, seed=seed + 31, verbose=verbose)
        s3 = local_score_arr(a3)
        if np.isfinite(s3) and s3 < best_score:
            best = a3.copy()
            best_score = s3

    return best


def propose_candidate(budget_s=1800.0):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    budget = float(min(budget_s, 1800.0))
    reserve = 12.0
    if budget <= reserve + 8.0:
        n_fb = 3000
        return [1.0 / float(n_fb)] * n_fb

    t_end = t_start + budget - reserve

    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    # Load parent construction if available.
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        p = p[np.isfinite(p)]
        if p.ndim == 1 and p.size >= 100 and p.sum() > 1e-10:
            cand = normalize_nonneg(p)
            sc = local_score_arr(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                best_seq = cand
                best_score = sc
                print(f"Parent loaded: n={len(best_seq)}, local_score={best_score:.6f}")
    except Exception:
        pass

    if best_seq is None:
        n0 = 5000
        best_seq = best_parametric_seed(n0, 15.0, seed=7)
        best_score = local_score_arr(best_seq)
        print(f"Default parametric seed: n={len(best_seq)}, score={best_score:.6f}")

    base_n = int(len(best_seq))

    # Candidate lengths.  Larger length often helps, but avoid excessive runtime.
    if base_n <= 8000:
        raw_lengths = [base_n, 8000, 10000, 12000]
    elif base_n <= 10000:
        raw_lengths = [base_n, 10000, 12000]
    else:
        raw_lengths = [base_n, max(8000, int(base_n * 0.85)), 12000]

    lengths = []
    for nn in raw_lengths:
        nn = int(max(3000, min(14000, nn)))
        if nn >= 2000 and nn not in lengths:
            lengths.append(nn)

    # First length must be the best parent length if present.
    if base_n not in lengths:
        lengths.insert(0, base_n)

    total_time_for_lengths = (t_end - t_start) * 0.82
    weights = [0.40, 0.28, 0.20, 0.12]

    for idx, nn in enumerate(lengths[:4]):
        now = time.monotonic()
        raw_alloc = total_time_for_lengths * (weights[idx] if idx < len(weights) else weights[-1])
        alloc = min(raw_alloc, t_end - now - 25.0)
        if alloc < 45.0:
            break

        if best_seq is not None:
            if len(best_seq) == nn:
                a_init = best_seq.copy()
            else:
                a_init = interpolate_sequence(best_seq, nn)
        else:
            a_init = best_parametric_seed(nn, min(20.0, alloc * 0.2), seed=100 + nn)

        sc_init = local_score_arr(a_init)
        if not np.isfinite(sc_init):
            a_init = np.ones(nn, dtype=np.float64) / float(nn)
            sc_init = local_score_arr(a_init)

        # Try a fresh structured seed and blend/replace if it is better.
        seed_time = min(25.0, alloc * 0.10)
        if seed_time >= 5.0:
            a_seed = best_parametric_seed(nn, seed_time, seed=2000 + nn)
            sc_seed = local_score_arr(a_seed)
            if np.isfinite(sc_seed) and sc_seed < sc_init:
                a_init = a_seed
                sc_init = sc_seed

        # Sometimes a blend of parent and seed escapes bad local basins.
        if best_seq is not None and len(best_seq) != nn:
            a_bl = 0.5 * a_init + 0.5 * interpolate_sequence(best_seq, nn)
            a_bl = normalize_nonneg(a_bl)
            sc_bl = local_score_arr(a_bl)
            if np.isfinite(sc_bl) and sc_bl < sc_init:
                a_init = a_bl
                sc_init = sc_bl

        print(f"\nLength phase n={nn}, initial_score={sc_init:.6f}, alloc={alloc:.0f}s")
        a_out = run_optimizer_phases(a_init, alloc, seed=500 + idx, verbose=True, include_var=True)
        sc_out = local_score_arr(a_out)
        if np.isfinite(sc_out) and sc_out < best_score:
            best_score = sc_out
            best_seq = a_out.copy()
            print(f"  *** NEW BEST n={nn}: score={best_score:.6f}")

    # Final polish on the best found length.
    rem = t_end - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 35.0 and best_seq is not None:
        print(f"\nFinal polish, remaining={rem:.0f}s")
        a_fin = run_optimizer_phases(best_seq, max(rem - 15.0, 0.0),
                                     seed=999, verbose=True, include_var=False)
        sc_fin = local_score_arr(a_fin)
        if np.isfinite(sc_fin) and sc_fin < best_score:
            best_score = sc_fin
            best_seq = a_fin.copy()
            print(f"  *** NEW BEST final: score={best_score:.6f}")

    # Very short pair-descent squeeze.
    rem = t_end - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 25.0 and best_seq is not None:
        pair_time = min(rem - 12.0, 90.0)
        if pair_time > 10.0:
            print(f"\nPair descent, time={pair_time:.0f}s")
            a_pair = pair_descent(best_seq, pair_time, seed=1234, verbose=True)
            sc_pair = local_score_arr(a_pair)
            if np.isfinite(sc_pair) and sc_pair < best_score:
                best_score = sc_pair
                best_seq = a_pair.copy()
                print(f"  *** NEW BEST pair: score={best_score:.6f}")

    # Ensure final vector is clean and non-negative.
    best_seq = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if best_seq.size == 0:
        best_seq = np.ones(5000, dtype=np.float64) / 5000.0
    best_seq = np.where(np.isfinite(best_seq), best_seq, 0.0)
    best_seq = np.clip(best_seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(best_seq.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        n_fb = 5000
        best_seq = np.ones(n_fb, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_fb)
        s = 1.0
    best_seq = best_seq / s

    final_local = local_score_arr(best_seq)
    print(f"\nFINAL local score={final_local:.6f}, n={len(best_seq)}, time={time.monotonic()-t_start:.0f}s")

    # Optional official validation if there is ample time.  The judge recomputes anyway.
    try:
        if t_end - time.monotonic() > 25.0 and len(best_seq) <= 13000:
            official = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best_seq]))
            if np.isfinite(official):
                print(f"Official evaluate_sequence score={official:.6f}")
    except Exception:
        pass

    return [float(x) for x in best_seq]
```
